Bessarabian Village Birth Records, 184x (R. Drefs)

        Published by the Odessa Digital Library - 15 Mar 1998
             http://www.odessa3.org

        This document may be freely used for personal, nonprofit
        purposes or linked by other WWW sites.  It may also be
        shared with others, provided the header with copyright
        notice is included.  However, it may not be republished
        in any form without permission of the copyright owner.

        Copyright 1998, Reuben R. Drefs

        This file contains birth records from the Bessarabian villages
        of South Russia for the years 1840 through 1848.  No records are 
        available for 1844 and 1849. This information was compiled by 
        R. Reuben Drefs, coordinator/extractor, extractors/data
        inputters Elli Wise, Anna Graumann, Dennis Eisenbeis, and
        volunteers.  From the St. Petersburg Lutheran Evangelical
        archives published by the LDS.  Edited by Marty McMahon, updated
        11-19-98.
